FROM appointments AS a
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u.user_id, u.created_at, u.date_of_birth, u.email, u.full_name, u.gender, u.is_verified, u.last_login, u.otp_attempts, u.otp_code, u.otp_expires_at, u.password_hash, u.phone_number, u.profile_image_url, u.status, u.updated_at, u.user_type, p.address, p.allergies, p.blood_type, p.chronic_conditions, p.current_medications, p.emergency_contact_name, p.emergency_contact_phone, p.medical_history
    FROM users AS u
    INNER JOIN patients AS p ON u.user_id = p.user_id
) AS s ON a.patient_id = s.user_id
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u0.user_id, u0.created_at, u0.date_of_birth, u0.email, u0.full_name, u0.gender, u0.is_verified, u0.last_login, u0.otp_attempts, u0.otp_code, u0.otp_expires_at, u0.password_hash, u0.phone_number, u0.profile_image_url, u0.status, u0.updated_at, u0.user_type, d.badge_number, d.languages_spoken, d.license_number, d.qualifications, d.specialization, d.years_of_experience
    FROM users AS u0
    INNER JOIN doctors AS d ON u0.user_id = d.user_id
) AS s0 ON a.doctor_id = s0.user_id
LEFT JOIN queue AS q ON a.appointment_id = q.appointment_id
WHERE a.center_id = @centerId AND a.appointment_date = @date
ORDER BY a.appointment_time

INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u.user_id, u.created_at, u.date_of_birth, u.email, u.full_name, u.gender, u.is_verified, u.last_login, u.otp_attempts, u.otp_code, u.otp_expires_at, u.password_hash, u.phone_number, u.profile_image_url, u.status, u.updated_at, u.user_type, p.address, p.allergies, p.blood_type, p.chronic_conditions, p.current_medications, p.emergency_contact_name, p.emergency_contact_phone, p.medical_history
    FROM users AS u
    INNER JOIN patients AS p ON u.user_id = p.user_id
) AS s ON a.patient_id = s.user_id
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u0.user_id, u0.created_at, u0.date_of_birth, u0.email, u0.full_name, u0.gender, u0.is_verified, u0.last_login, u0.otp_attempts, u0.otp_code, u0.otp_expires_at, u0.password_hash, u0.phone_number, u0.profile_image_url, u0.status, u0.updated_at, u0.user_type, d.badge_number, d.languages_spoken, d.license_number, d.qualifications, d.specialization, d.years_of_experience
    FROM users AS u0
    INNER JOIN doctors AS d ON u0.user_id = d.user_id
) AS s0 ON a.doctor_id = s0.user_id
LEFT JOIN queue AS q ON a.appointment_id = q.appointment_id
WHERE a.center_id = @centerId AND a.appointment_date = @date
ORDER BY a.appointment_time

INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u.user_id, u.created_at, u.date_of_birth, u.email, u.full_name, u.gender, u.is_verified, u.last_login, u.otp_attempts, u.otp_code, u.otp_expires_at, u.password_hash, u.phone_number, u.profile_image_url, u.status, u.updated_at, u.user_type, p.address, p.allergies, p.blood_type, p.chronic_conditions, p.current_medications, p.emergency_contact_name, p.emergency_contact_phone, p.medical_history
    FROM users AS u
    INNER JOIN patients AS p ON u.user_id = p.user_id
) AS s ON a.patient_id = s.user_id
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u0.user_id, u0.created_at, u0.date_of_birth, u0.email, u0.full_name, u0.gender, u0.is_verified, u0.last_login, u0.otp_attempts, u0.otp_code, u0.otp_expires_at, u0.password_hash, u0.phone_number, u0.profile_image_url, u0.status, u0.updated_at, u0.user_type, d.badge_number, d.languages_spoken, d.license_number, d.qualifications, d.specialization, d.years_of_experience
    FROM users AS u0
    INNER JOIN doctors AS d ON u0.user_id = d.user_id
) AS s0 ON a.doctor_id = s0.user_id
LEFT JOIN queue AS q ON a.appointment_id = q.appointment_id

    SELECT u.user_id, u.created_at, u.date_of_birth, u.email, u.full_name, u.gender, u.is_verified, u.last_login, u.otp_attempts, u.otp_code, u.otp_expires_at, u.password_hash, u.phone_number, u.profile_image_url, u.status, u.updated_at, u.user_type, p.address, p.allergies, p.blood_type, p.chronic_conditions, p.current_medications, p.emergency_contact_name, p.emergency_contact_phone, p.medical_history
    FROM users AS u
    INNER JOIN patients AS p ON u.user_id = p.user_id
) AS s ON a.patient_id = s.user_id
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT u0.user_id, u0.created_at, u0.date_of_birth, u0.email, u0.full_name, u0.gender, u0.is_verified, u0.last_login, u0.otp_attempts, u0.otp_code, u0.otp_expires_at, u0.password_hash, u0.phone_number, u0.profile_image_url, u0.status, u0.updated_at, u0.user_type, d.badge_number, d.languages_spoken, d.license_number, d.qualifications, d.specialization, d.years_of_experience
    FROM users AS u0
    INNER JOIN doctors AS d ON u0.user_id = d.user_id
) AS s0 ON a.doctor_id = s0.user_id
LEFT JOIN queue AS q ON a.appointment_id = q.appointment_id
